# LDO-CORE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Shared external measurements: LDO-CORE-2026-09-29

These definitions apply to both formal task cards. The implementation must be
checked natively with the pinned models; offline algebra is not that evidence.
Do not inherit the old fixed-OTA threshold or case-count definitions.

## DC: port signs, cost and absolute voltage

All four DUT ports have zero-volt meters. Load and decap return to external node
`0`, not the DUT side of Vground. Thus the regulator ground-current measurement
does not include delivered load current.

`Iin=i(Vpin)`, `Iref=i(Vrefmeter)` enter VIN/VREF;
`Iout=i(Voutmeter)`, `Ignd=i(Vground)` leave VOUT/VSS.
Check `Iin+Iref-Iout-Ignd` against an absolute 1 nA tolerance. A failed residual is invalid measurement,
not a favorable low-current result. Save signed
currents, voltages and OP status. The DC overhead is
`max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0)`, with a separate `abs(Iref)<=1 uA` gate. Reference
sourcing is charged, and sinking is already reflected in Iin-Iout. This is not
no-load IQ. Apply the minimum-load cap only at the minimum load, and the general
cap at all DC loads. Missing currents or invalid OP cannot become zero overhead.
Dissipation is `VIN*Iin+VREF*Iref-VOUT*Iout`, using VSS-relative voltages. Keep it
as characterization; input power contains useful load power. No dynamic-energy
score and no DC-overhead interpretation of capacitor charging during transients.

Measure error from each card's fixed target, not a candidate's own output:
SKY130 DC/recovery band is 1.176-1.224 V, envelope 1.104-1.296 V;
ADM45 DC/recovery band is 0.784-0.816 V, envelope 0.736-0.864 V.
Final verification evaluates independent DC solutions at every required load
and VIN condition. A transient tail is not a DC solution. Exploration may request
a single transient without separate DC cases: recovery uses the fixed target,
and the transient deck records its own operating point and initial hold. Such a
request does not establish DC compliance at every load.
Line/load regulation and actual signed currents remain available for diagnosis.

## Prescribed loads and windows

| Profile | Levels | Time definition |
|---|---|---|
| light_heavy | card minimum -> maximum -> minimum | 1 us pre-hold, 100 ns edges, 2 us high/low plateaus |
| mid_heavy | card middle -> maximum -> middle | same |
| burst | minimum <-> maximum, 8 pulses | 100 ns edges, 200 ns high/low plateaus, 1 us extra final tail |

SKY130 levels are 50 uA/0.5 mA/2 mA; ADM45 levels 25 uA/0.25 mA/1 mA.
PWL loads are explicit electrical stimuli, not data from an RTL block. Reference
and VIN are fixed in load tests. The regulator stays enabled; start from its DC
solution without `.ic`/`uic`. The 1 us pre-hold must also satisfy the DC voltage
band; do not hide pre-existing oscillation by starting the score at the edge.

Recovery starts at each isolated edge START, not its end. Interpolate the last
entry into the target +/-2% band which stays in the band until the next edge
or record end. Require <=500 ns. Record extrema and absolute target-relative
errors during the whole transition/window. A flat but wrong voltage fails.
A complete trace ending outside the band has null recovery and an observation-
time lower bound; a bound exceeding the limit is MISS, not missing measurement.
Short/nonfinite/unordered/insufficiently sampled records are invalid evidence.

For bursts score the absolute envelope through **all** pulses, then recovery
only after the final falling edge. Do not apply a 500 ns per-edge settling
requirement to a 200 ns plateau. Total burst duration is 6.8 us, including its
extra tail. Record peak-to-peak output as characterization, not a new stability
score. Isolated profiles are 5.2 us. Positive and negative excursions both count.

Maximum timestep and requested output step are 0.5 ns. Check stored sample gaps
against 0.5 ns with relative slack 1e-9, using actual timestamps rather than
assuming the requested output grid was delivered. Qualify convergence and compare
representative extremes with a finer grid before release. This is finite-window,
sampled evidence, not proof against every internal or unobserved oscillation.

## Line and PSRR: mandatory characterization, not scoring

Line: use each card's VIN minimum -> maximum -> minimum, 200 ns edges, maximum
load, reference fixed. Report the same target-relative windows. PSRR: valid
nominal OP at minimum and maximum load; VIN AC=1, reference/load AC=0.
`PSRR=20*log10(abs(VIN_ac/VOUT_ac))`. Save actual phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and
the intervening sweep. This is supply-to-output rejection, not the OTA signal-
normalized ratio. Zero/underflow/nonfinite transfer is unresolved/invalid, not
infinite PASS. A non-regulating or oscillating state cannot provide a qualified
PSRR result merely because an AC command ran.

## Models and accounting

The host selects the pinned SKY130 continuous `.lib` section or ADM45 generated
physical instances. ADM45 slow/fast uses process-only generation with the card's
fixed profile, seed and sample. Temperature is passed to the generator and bench;
the saved process values stay fixed across temperature, supply and load.

Each deck uses one native invocation, including its OP and subsequent
AC/tran commands. Standalone exploration transients cost one case each, including their operating
point and waveform. Separate DC requests cost one case per load point or three
for the complete DC set. The full nominal request costs six cases.
The formal plan is 18 public groups x 6 cases, 3 independent groups x 6 cases,
and 3 characterization cases = 129 native invocations. Each six-case group has
three DC destination OP cases plus light_heavy, mid_heavy and burst transients.
Initial OPs inside transient processes do not replace final verification DC cases.
The complete matrix is generated by the shared task planner.
Shared results can be reused only when the existing controller proves identity;
no cross-group count reduction is part of this specification. Keep every failed
or interrupted invocation; no free retries and no early stop on electrical MISS.
Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

Method sources (not support for our unmeasured numerical thresholds):
- https://ngspice.sourceforge.io/ngspice-control-language-tutorial.html
- https://www.analog.com/en/resources/analog-dialogue/articles/understand-ldo-concepts.html
- ADM45 VTG units, multiplicity and synthetic stress semantics are specified in the task card.

## Verdict and observation completeness

Apply DC accuracy, current limits and KCL only to valid native OP records.
For each full transient, require the complete initial hold to be within the
absolute +/-2% band, and the post-edge envelope to stay within +/-8%. Isolated
profiles must recover by 500 ns from each edge start. Bursts apply recovery only
after the final falling edge. These are independent requirements: recovery does
not excuse a peak outside the envelope. Do not round values before inclusive
limit checks. A null recovery on a complete waveform carries its lower bound;
truncated, nonfinite, nonmonotone or overly sparse waveforms are invalid evidence.

Public and independent electrical cases must all have valid passing results for
an electrical PASS. Retain individual failing rows, observed waveforms and
execution status; do not turn incomplete execution into a completed electrical
MISS. Characterization is attempted and reported even when required metrics fail,
where simulation is possible; its unavailable results remain explicit.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |
| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| light_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | light_heavy |
| light_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | light_heavy |
| mid_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | mid_heavy |
| mid_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | mid_heavy |

| psrr_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | psrr_low |
| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |
| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- light_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- burst: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- line: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

Bash
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())
MODEL

The first circuit is valid and uses 1,630 µm² of the 5,000 µm² MOS area limit. It uses a PMOS pass device, a resistive feedback divider, and a small differential amplifier. I’ll measure DC regulation first, then check load transients if it regulates.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_direct.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for m in (4,8,16,32):
 for c in (2,5,10,20):
  design=f'''* Single-stage PMOS LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RTAIL VIN PT 6k
XINFB NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XINRF GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m={m}
CCOMP GATE VOUT {c}p
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  eid=f'sw_m{m}_c{c}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'light_heavy','pvt':'N','cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(p.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(p.stdout)
   case=x['cases'][0];v=case['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows']
   print(eid,case['status'],round(e['droop_v']*1e3),round(e['overshoot_v']*1e3),[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),p.stdout[:300],flush=True)

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_twostage.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for m in (4,8,16):
 for c in (3,7,15):
  design=f'''* Two-stage PMOS-input LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RTAIL VIN PT 6k
XINRF NDI VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XINFB CTRL FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 CTRL NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
RUP VIN GATE 50k
XDRIVE GATE CTRL VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=4
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m={m}
CMILL CTRL GATE {c}p
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  eid=f'ts_m{m}_c{c}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'light_heavy','pvt':'N','cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(p.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(p.stdout)
   case=x['cases'][0];v=case['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows'];hold=v['initial_hold']
   print(eid,case['status'],'hold',round((hold['max_v']-hold['min_v'])*1e3),'droop',round(e['droop_v']*1e3),'overshoot',round(e['overshoot_v']*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),p.stdout[:300],flush=True)

MODEL

The single-stage sweep shows the same pattern across pass sizes: load-step droop stays near 0.25 V. The two-stage version reduces the first droop somewhat but overshoots badly when the gate cannot recharge quickly. I’m testing a complementary gate driver so it can move the pass gate in both directions.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_driver.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for w in (5,10,20,40,80):
 for r in (20,50,100):
  design=f'''* Two-stage PMOS-input LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RTAIL VIN PT 6k
XINRF NDI VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XINFB CTRL FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 CTRL NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
RUP VIN GATE {r}k
XDRIVE GATE CTRL VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={w} l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=8
CCOMP GATE VOUT 5p
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  eid=f'drv_w{w}_r{r}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'light_heavy','pvt':'N','cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(p.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(p.stdout)
   case=x['cases'][0];v=case['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows'];hold=v['initial_hold']
   print(eid,case['status'],'iq',round(v['overhead_a']*1e6),'hold',round((hold['max_v']-hold['min_v'])*1e3),'droop',round(e['droop_v']*1e3),'overshoot',round(e['overshoot_v']*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),p.stdout[:300],flush=True)

MODEL

The divider’s resistance matters: lowering it improves both droop and recovery, but the divider current then uses much of the light-load current allowance. I’m adding a small capacitor across the upper feedback resistor to make the sense node follow fast output changes while keeping the DC divider current low.

.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF

The tuned single-stage loop now passes nominal isolated load steps, but its burst peak reaches 147 mV and its amplifier current rises sharply at high VIN and temperature. I’m replacing the simple source resistor with a mirrored PMOS bias to keep the amplifier current closer across corners.

* PMOS LDO with mirrored input-pair bias and fast feedback.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_mirror.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for cf in (2,5,10,15):
 for cc in (0.5,1):
  design=f'''* Mirrored-bias single-stage PMOS LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB {cf}p
RBIAS PB VSS 130k
XPBIAS PB PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XTAIL PT PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=16
XINFB NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=4
XINRF GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=4
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=8
CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for a in ('light_heavy','burst'):
   eid=f'mir_f{cf}_c{str(cc).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{a}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':'N','cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
   p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(p.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(p.stdout)
    case=x['cases'][0];v=case['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows'];hold=v['initial_hold']
    print(eid,case['status'],'droop',round(e['droop_v']*1e3),'overshoot',round(e['overshoot_v']*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),p.stdout[:300],flush=True)

MODEL

The mirrored bias reduces current variation, but its load recovery and burst behavior are worse. The client reports about 4,647 model seconds remaining. I’m keeping the simpler amplifier and testing a shorter pass transistor, which should reduce gate charge and let the same bias current move it faster.

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for m in (8,16,32):
 for cf in (1,5,10):
  for cc in (0,0.5):
   design=f'''* Single-stage PMOS LDO with low-gate-charge pass device
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB {cf}p
RTAIL VIN PT 6k
XINFB NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XINRF GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m={m}
{f'CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p' if cc else ''}
.ends DUT
'''
   Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
   for a in ('light_heavy','burst'):
    eid=f'pass_m{m}_f{cf}_c{str(cc).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{a}'
    Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':'N','cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
    try:
     x=json.loads(p.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(p.stdout)
     case=x['cases'][0];v=case['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows'];hold=v['initial_hold']
     print(eid,case['status'],'iq',round(v['overhead_a']*1e6),'droop',round(e['droop_v']*1e3),'overshoot',round(e['overshoot_v']*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
    except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),p.stdout[:300],flush=True)

MODEL

The short pass device improves nominal transient response, but its leakage pushes the fast, hot, high-VIN light-load output too high. The slow, cold, low-VIN amplifier also has only about 2 µA available and responds too slowly. I’m testing a resistor and diode based bias that should hold amplifier current more evenly, with a slightly longer pass device to reduce leakage.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_input.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for l in (0.15,0.18,0.25,0.35,0.5):
 design=f'''* Direct PMOS-input LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 1p
RTAIL VIN PT 6k
XINFB NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l={l}
XINRF GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l={l}
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.25 m=8
.ends DUT
'''
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FHH'):
  eid=f'in_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}'
  a='nominal' if p=='SLC' else 'dc'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases'][:3]]
   print(eid,'dc',[round(c['vout_v'],3) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6),flush=True)
   if p=='SLC':
    for c in x['cases'][3:]:
     v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

MODEL

A useful cause emerged: the earlier window controller sensed output through 300 kΩ dividers, so its inputs could lag a fast edge. I’m retrying that controller with capacitive sensing and smaller gate drivers. This should let each driver release the pass gate as soon as output returns toward the target.

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for l in (0.18,0.2,0.22,0.25):
 design=f'''* NMOS-input LDO with supply feedforward
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RLINE VIN FB 9meg
CFF VOUT FB 1p
RTAIL NT VSS 5k
XNFB PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNREF GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XP1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XP2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l={l} m=8
.ends DUT
'''
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FHH'):
  eid=f'np_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases'][:3]]
   print(eid,'dc',[round(c['vout_v'],3) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6),'failed',[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
   for c in x['cases'][3:]:
    v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

The NMOS loop is close on envelope at nominal and fast/hot conditions. At the slow/cold corner, the output crosses the recovery band repeatedly before settling, so I’m tuning feedforward and gate compensation to damp that response. /bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_ncomp.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for cf in (0.3,0.5,1,2):
 for cc in (0,0.2,0.5):
  design=f'''* NMOS-input LDO compensation sweep
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RLINE VIN FB 9meg
CFF VOUT FB {cf}p
RTAIL NT VSS 5k
XNFB PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNREF GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XP1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XP2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.2 m=8
{f'CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p' if cc else ''}
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for p,a in (('N','light_heavy'),('SLC','light_heavy'),('SLC','burst')):
   eid=f'nc_f{str(cf).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_c{str(cc).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}_{a}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
   r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
    v=x['cases'][0]['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows']
    print(eid,'v',round(v['vout_v'],3),'iq',round(v['overhead_a']*1e6),'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_pass_short.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for l in (0.15,0.16,0.17,0.18,0.19,0.2):
 design=f'''* NMOS-input LDO
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RLINE VIN FB 9meg
CFF VOUT FB 2p
RTAIL NT VSS 5k
XNFB PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNREF GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XP1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XP2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l={l} m=8
CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p
.ends DUT
'''
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FHH'):
  eid=f'ps_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}'
  a='nominal' if p"'!='"'FHH' else 'dc'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases'][:3]]
   print(eid,'dc',[round(c['vout_v'],3) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6),'failed',[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
   if p"'!='"'FHH':
    for c in x['cases'][3:]:
     v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('dut.spice').read_text()
for rb in (1000,500,300,250,200):
 design=base.replace('.ends DUT',f'RBLEED VOUT VSS {rb}k\\n.ends DUT')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p,a in (('FHH','dc'),('N','nominal'),('SLC','nominal')):
  eid=f'bleed_r{rb}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases'][:3]]
   print(eid,'dc',[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2),'failed',[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
   if a=='nominal':
    for c in x['cases'][3:]:
     v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
 base=base

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_leak.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for l in (0.3,0.4,0.5):
 for m in (8,12,16):
  design=f'''* NMOS-input LDO pass leakage study
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 1p
RTAIL NT VSS 5k
XNFB PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNREF GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XP1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XP2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l={l} m={m}
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for p in ('N','SLC','FLH','FHH'):
   eid=f'leak_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_m{m}_{p}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
   r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
    dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
    print(eid,'dc',[round(c['vout_v'],3) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6),flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
PY

MODEL

* Complementary-input single-output PMOS LDO.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 2p
RPT VIN PT 20k
XPF NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XPR GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XNM1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XNM2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
RNT NT VSS 10k
XNF PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNR GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XPM1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPM2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.18 m=4
CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p
.ends DUT

import json,subprocess
for p in ('N','SLC','FLH','FHH'):
 q={'action':'measure','experiment_id':'m023_'+p,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10}
 open('REQUEST.json','w').write(json.dumps(q)+'\\n')
 r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
 x=json.loads(r.stdout);open('m023_'+p+'.json','w').write(r.stdout)
 print(p,'dc',[(round(c['measurements']['vout_v'],4),round(c['measurements']['overhead_a']*1e6,2)) for c in x['cases'][:3]],'fails',[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
 for c in x['cases'][3:]:
  v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
PY"

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_pmc.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for cc in (0.2,0.5,1,2,5,10):
 design=f'''* PMOS LDO compensation study
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 2p
RBIAS VIN NB 160k
XNBIAS NB NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XNSINK PB NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPDI PB PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XTAIL PT PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=12
XINFB NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=4
XINRF GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 m=4
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.18 m=4
CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p
.ends DUT
'''
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p,a in (('N','burst'),('SLC','light_heavy'),('SLC','burst'),('FLH','light_heavy')):
  eid=f'pmc_c{str(cc).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}_{a}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   v=x['cases'][0]['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows']
   print(eid,'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
PY
python3 /workspace/sweep_pmc.py"

pmc_c10_FLH_light_heavy pk 219 rec [741, 1435]
MODEL

The parallel loop and capacitive auxiliary loop both preserve DC accuracy but still miss slow/cold load steps. I’m testing a locally damped push-pull gate driver next. It gives the error amplifier a fast way to charge and discharge the small pass gate; the local resistor is intended to keep that driver from latching at a rail.

0:57:08
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/dut.spice <<'EOF'
* Two-stage PMOS-input LDO with locally damped gate driver.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 2p
RTAIL VIN PT 15k
XINRF NDI VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XINFB CTRL FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XMN1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XMN2 CTRL NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XDN GATE CTRL VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=0.15
XUP GATE CTRL VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=0.15
RLOCAL CTRL GATE 100k
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.18 m=4
CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p
.ends DUT
EOF

MODEL

The full 200 pF sweep now has valid DC in eight of nine corners and full passes in three. Several remaining transient misses are late recovery crossings after modest peaks; slow/cold and slow/high-VIN still exceed the envelope. I’m sweeping gate compensation to damp the long recovery without losing the fast response.

1:05:14
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_combo_comp.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('best_m027.spice').read_text()
for cc in (0.1,0.4,0.8,1.5,3):
 design=base.replace('CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p',f'CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','SHC','SHH','FHC'):
  eid=f'cmb_c{str(cc).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   fs=[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']"'!='"'PASS']
   print(eid,'fails',fs,flush=True)
   for c in x['cases'][3:]:
    v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
PY

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_diode.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('dut.spice').read_text()
for l in (0.15,0.25):
 for rs in (0,100,300,1000):
  if rs:
   design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT NC VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l={l}\\nRNC NC FB {rs}k')
  else:
   design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l={l}')
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for p in ('N','SLC','FLC','FLH','FHH'):
   eid=f'dio_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_r{rs}_{p}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
   r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
    dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
    print(eid,[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2),flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
PY

from pathlib import Path

  for p in ('N','SLC','FLC','FLH','FHH'):

MODEL

The short PMOS diode corrects fast/cold output, but it overcorrects fast/hot by much more; a series resistor does not balance that difference enough. I’m measuring a temperature-sensitive sink at the sense node so I can combine the two currents and keep their net trim closer across temperature.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_sink.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('best_m028.spice').read_text().replace('l=0.18 m=4','l=0.18 m=3')
for w,l in ((0.42,0.15),(0.42,0.25),(0.42,0.5),(0.42,1),(0.42,2),(1,1),(2,1)):
 design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5\\nXNSHUNT FB FB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={w} l={l}')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FLC','FLH','FHH'):
  eid=f'sink_w{str(w).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
   print(eid,[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2),flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

MODEL

The source-node sink does not isolate the fast/cold case enough: it also shifts nominal and fast/hot substantially. I’m testing a diode fed from VIN into the sense node through a large resistor. At 1.4 V its gate overdrive may distinguish fast from slow silicon; the resistor limits its current at 1.8 V.

Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > /workspace/sweep_vindiode.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('best_m028.spice').read_text().replace('l=0.18 m=4','l=0.18 m=3')
for l,r in ((0.15,1000),(0.15,2000),(0.15,5000),(0.15,10000),(0.5,1000),(0.5,5000)):
 design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5\\nRVCOMP VIN NV {r}k\\nXNVCOMP NV NV FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l={l}')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FLC','FLH','FHH','SHC'):
  eid=f'vdi_l{str(l).replace(\".\",\"d\")}_r{r}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\\n')
  rr=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(rr.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(rr.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
   print(eid,[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2),flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),rr.stdout[:200],flush=True)

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

# LDO-CORE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

## Time accounting

# Shared external measurements: LDO-CORE-2026-09-29

These definitions apply to both formal task cards. The implementation must be
checked natively with the pinned models; offline algebra is not that evidence.
Do not inherit the old fixed-OTA threshold or case-count definitions.

## DC: port signs, cost and absolute voltage

All four DUT ports have zero-volt meters. Load and decap return to external node
`0`, not the DUT side of Vground. Thus the regulator ground-current measurement
does not include delivered load current.

`Iin=i(Vpin)`, `Iref=i(Vrefmeter)` enter VIN/VREF;
`Iout=i(Voutmeter)`, `Ignd=i(Vground)` leave VOUT/VSS.
Check `Iin+Iref-Iout-Ignd` against an absolute 1 nA tolerance. A failed residual is invalid measurement,
not a favorable low-current result. Save signed
currents, voltages and OP status. The DC overhead is
`max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0)`, with a separate `abs(Iref)<=1 uA` gate. Reference
sourcing is charged, and sinking is already reflected in Iin-Iout. This is not
no-load IQ. Apply the minimum-load cap only at the minimum load, and the general
cap at all DC loads. Missing currents or invalid OP cannot become zero overhead.
Dissipation is `VIN*Iin+VREF*Iref-VOUT*Iout`, using VSS-relative voltages. Keep it
as characterization; input power contains useful load power. No dynamic-energy
score and no DC-overhead interpretation of capacitor charging during transients.

Measure error from each card's fixed target, not a candidate's own output:
SKY130 DC/recovery band is 1.176-1.224 V, envelope 1.104-1.296 V;
ADM45 DC/recovery band is 0.784-0.816 V, envelope 0.736-0.864 V.
Final verification evaluates independent DC solutions at every required load
and VIN condition. A transient tail is not a DC solution. Exploration may request
a single transient without separate DC cases: recovery uses the fixed target,
and the transient deck records its own operating point and initial hold. Such a
request does not establish DC compliance at every load.
Line/load regulation and actual signed currents remain available for diagnosis.

## Prescribed loads and windows

| Profile | Levels | Time definition |
|---|---|---|
| light_heavy | card minimum -> maximum -> minimum | 1 us pre-hold, 100 ns edges, 2 us high/low plateaus |
| mid_heavy | card middle -> maximum -> middle | same |
| burst | minimum <-> maximum, 8 pulses | 100 ns edges, 200 ns high/low plateaus, 1 us extra final tail |

SKY130 levels are 50 uA/0.5 mA/2 mA; ADM45 levels 25 uA/0.25 mA/1 mA.
PWL loads are explicit electrical stimuli, not data from an RTL block. Reference
and VIN are fixed in load tests. The regulator stays enabled; start from its DC
solution without `.ic`/`uic`. The 1 us pre-hold must also satisfy the DC voltage
band; do not hide pre-existing oscillation by starting the score at the edge.

Recovery starts at each isolated edge START, not its end. Interpolate the last
entry into the target +/-2% band which stays in the band until the next edge
or record end. Require <=500 ns. Record extrema and absolute target-relative
errors during the whole transition/window. A flat but wrong voltage fails.
A complete trace ending outside the band has null recovery and an observation-
time lower bound; a bound exceeding the limit is MISS, not missing measurement.
Short/nonfinite/unordered/insufficiently sampled records are invalid evidence.

For bursts score the absolute envelope through **all** pulses, then recovery
only after the final falling edge. Do not apply a 500 ns per-edge settling
requirement to a 200 ns plateau. Total burst duration is 6.8 us, including its
extra tail. Record peak-to-peak output as characterization, not a new stability
score. Isolated profiles are 5.2 us. Positive and negative excursions both count.

Maximum timestep and requested output step are 0.5 ns. Check stored sample gaps
against 0.5 ns with relative slack 1e-9, using actual timestamps rather than
assuming the requested output grid was delivered. Qualify convergence and compare
representative extremes with a finer grid before release. This is finite-window,
sampled evidence, not proof against every internal or unobserved oscillation.

## Line and PSRR: mandatory characterization, not scoring

Line: use each card's VIN minimum -> maximum -> minimum, 200 ns edges, maximum
load, reference fixed. Report the same target-relative windows. PSRR: valid
nominal OP at minimum and maximum load; VIN AC=1, reference/load AC=0.
`PSRR=20*log10(abs(VIN_ac/VOUT_ac))`. Save actual phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and
the intervening sweep. This is supply-to-output rejection, not the OTA signal-
normalized ratio. Zero/underflow/nonfinite transfer is unresolved/invalid, not
infinite PASS. A non-regulating or oscillating state cannot provide a qualified
PSRR result merely because an AC command ran.

## Models and accounting

The host selects the pinned SKY130 continuous `.lib` section or ADM45 generated
physical instances. ADM45 slow/fast uses process-only generation with the card's
fixed profile, seed and sample. Temperature is passed to the generator and bench;
the saved process values stay fixed across temperature, supply and load.

Each deck uses one native invocation, including its OP and subsequent
AC/tran commands. Standalone exploration transients cost one case each, including their operating
point and waveform. Separate DC requests cost one case per load point or three
for the complete DC set. The full nominal request costs six cases.
The formal plan is 18 public groups x 6 cases, 3 independent groups x 6 cases,
and 3 characterization cases = 129 native invocations. Each six-case group has
three DC destination OP cases plus light_heavy, mid_heavy and burst transients.
Initial OPs inside transient processes do not replace final verification DC cases.
The complete matrix is generated by the shared task planner.
Shared results can be reused only when the existing controller proves identity;
no cross-group count reduction is part of this specification. Keep every failed
or interrupted invocation; no free retries and no early stop on electrical MISS.
Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

Method sources (not support for our unmeasured numerical thresholds):
- https://ngspice.sourceforge.io/ngspice-control-language-tutorial.html
- https://www.analog.com/en/resources/analog-dialogue/articles/understand-ldo-concepts.html
- ADM45 VTG units, multiplicity and synthetic stress semantics are specified in the task card.

## Verdict and observation completeness

Apply DC accuracy, current limits and KCL only to valid native OP records.
For each full transient, require the complete initial hold to be within the
absolute +/-2% band, and the post-edge envelope to stay within +/-8%. Isolated
profiles must recover by 500 ns from each edge start. Bursts apply recovery only
after the final falling edge. These are independent requirements: recovery does
not excuse a peak outside the envelope. Do not round values before inclusive
limit checks. A null recovery on a complete waveform carries its lower bound;
truncated, nonfinite, nonmonotone or overly sparse waveforms are invalid evidence.

Public and independent electrical cases must all have valid passing results for
an electrical PASS. Retain individual failing rows, observed waveforms and
execution status; do not turn incomplete execution into a completed electrical
MISS. Characterization is attempted and reported even when required metrics fail,
where simulation is possible; its unavailable results remain explicit.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

Bash
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('best_m027.spice').read_text().replace('CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p','CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.4p')
for cf in (0.5,1,1.5,2.5,3.5,5):
 design=base.replace('CFF VOUT FB 2p',f'CFF VOUT FB {cf}p')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','SHC'):
  eid=f'cmbf_f{str(cf).replace(".","d")}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   fs=[(z['case_id'],z['metric']) for z in x['summary']['spec_table'] if z['verdict']!='PASS']
   print(eid,'fails',fs,flush=True)
   for c in x['cases'][3:]:
    v=c['measurements'];e=v['envelope'];print(' ',c['case_id'],'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in v['recovery_windows']],flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for cf in (0.3,0.5,1,2):
 for cc in (0,0.2,0.5):
  design=f'''* NMOS-input LDO compensation sweep
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
RLINE VIN FB 9meg
CFF VOUT FB {cf}p
RTAIL NT VSS 5k
XNFB PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNREF GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XP1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XP2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.2 m=8
{f'CCOMP GATE VOUT {cc}p' if cc else ''}
.ends DUT
'''
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for p,a in (('N','light_heavy'),('SLC','light_heavy'),('SLC','burst')):
   eid=f'nc_f{str(cf).replace(".","d")}_c{str(cc).replace(".","d")}_{p}_{a}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':a,'pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\n')
   r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
    v=x['cases'][0]['measurements'];e=v['envelope']; rw=v['recovery_windows']
    print(eid,'v',round(v['vout_v'],3),'iq',round(v['overhead_a']*1e6),'pk',round(max(e['droop_v'],e['overshoot_v'])*1e3),'rec',[round(z['recovery_s']*1e9) if z['recovery_s'] else None for z in rw],flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
base=Path('dut.spice').read_text()
for l in (0.15,0.25):
 for rs in (0,100,300,1000):
  if rs:
   design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT NC VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l={l}\nRNC NC FB {rs}k')
  else:
   design=base.replace('XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l=0.5',f'XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=0.42 l={l}')
  Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
  for p in ('N','SLC','FLC','FLH','FHH'):
   eid=f'dio_l{str(l).replace(".","d")}_r{rs}_{p}'
   Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\n')
   r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
   try:
    x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
    dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
    print(eid,[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6,2),flush=True)
   except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for w in (0.42,1,2,5,10,20,50):
 design=f'''* Complementary-input LDO with NMOS diode trim
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFBT VOUT FB 300k
RFBB FB VSS 300k
CFF VOUT FB 2p
XNCOMP VOUT VOUT FB VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={w} l=0.5
RPT VIN PT 20k
XPF NDI FB PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XPR GATE VREF PT VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5
XNM1 NDI NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
XNM2 GATE NDI VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1
RNT NT VSS 10k
XNF PDI FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XNR GATE VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
XPM1 PDI PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=2.5 l=0.5
XPM2 GATE PDI VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=4 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT GATE VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.18 m=4
CCOMP GATE VOUT 0.2p
.ends DUT
'''
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(design)
 for p in ('N','SLC','FLH','FHH'):
  eid=f'mc_w{str(w).replace(".","d")}_{p}'
  Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':eid,'analysis':'dc','pvt':p,'cl':2e-10})+'\n')
  r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:
   x=json.loads(r.stdout);Path(eid+'.json').write_text(r.stdout)
   dc=[c['measurements'] for c in x['cases']]
   print(eid,[round(c['vout_v'],4) for c in dc],'iq',round(dc[0]['overhead_a']*1e6),flush=True)
  except Exception as exc: print(eid,'ERR',str(exc),r.stdout[:200],flush=True)
